Wine 10 source clarifies `run_wineboot` reports a **nonzero NtCreateUserProcess return**; `NtCreateUserProcess` can also propagate the failing child-startup **exit_code** returned from wineserver `get_new_process_info`. Therefore `0x1` is a process-startup failure status, NOT evidence that the user-level Wineboot `--init` ran and returned exit 1. Root cause may involve Wine's native child loader re-exec; NOT yet proven. **NEW v0.79** makes an explicit, *separately confirmed* one-shot **direct Wineboot.exe `-i` test** from the v3-proven matched `aarch64-windows` PE location, with unchanged source-sealed modules, no global bootstrap-mode override, new **`charoite-next-wine29-direct-init-v4`** private scratch only, bounded process/logs, and checks for `drive_c` plus `system.reg` and `user.reg`. ## 4. Main unresolved functional issue — Battle.net endless spinner

Official Battle.net Windows installer was installed in dedicated **Battle.net Gaming** environment. Installer first crashed but subsequent install completed. The launcher/client displays a spinning interface indefinitely instead of finishing sign-in. The **root cause is unknown**.

**What was already implemented/tried, without a confirmed resolution:**
1. Original `Battle.net Launcher.exe`; direct `Battle.net.exe` startup bypassing bootstrapper.
2. Safe Browser `--disable-gpu`; opt-in WineD3D/VirGL vs DXVK/Vulkan and ESYNC/FSYNC off; session-only DLL `locationapi=d` and `WINE_SIMULATE_WRITECOPY=1`.
3. Explicit backed-up persisted `Battle.net.config` `Client.HardwareAcceleration=false`.
4. Read-only Agent logs, WinHTTP/DNS probe and same-session Agent/client process monitor.
5. New read-only CEF/browser preferences and categorized client logs; **optional reversible cache rename** added in last diagnostic build. Do **not** repeatedly reinstall launcher or delete Agent.

**Definitive monitored result from same active Wine session (16 samples):**
```
monitor_same_session=1
monitor_expected_samples=16
monitor_samples_completed=16
monitor_agent_present_samples=15
monitor_client_present_samples=16
monitor_launcher_present_samples=0
monitor_max_agent=1
monitor_max_client=5
monitor_complete=1
monitor_process_enumeration_error_samples=0
```
Agent.exe **does run** and client processes exist. The earlier standalone monitor reporting no Blizzard processes belonged to a *different* Wine activity and is **not** valid evidence of no Agent in the active session. Process presence alone does not prove Agent IPC, CEF renderer, login or authentication succeeds.

**Wine-side basic network probe:**
```
battle_net_dns_status=0
battle_net_https_phase=7
battle_net_https_win32_error=0
battle_net_http_status=200
downloader_dns_status=0
downloader_https_phase=7
downloader_https_win32_error=0
downloader_http_status=404
```
DNS/HTTPS work for tested endpoints. An HTTP 404 for downloader root is not necessarily an error. This does not prove auth endpoints, CDN completeness or Agent IPC.

**Most important next device evidence:**
- Go to **Battle.net Gaming → Setup → VIEW BATTLE.NET LOGIN / CEF REPORT** and share the report (designed not to expose secrets). It reads persisted hardware acceleration status and bounded aggregate counts for CEF/browser, GPU/compositor, Agent/IPC, network, crash/exception, and login/authentication.
- The larger **CHECK LAUNCHER + AGENT DIAGNOSTICS** and **VIEW LIVE AGENT MONITOR** remain available.
- Try the direct Safe Browser launch; note if still spinning. If desired, **fully close Battle.net first**, then explicitly confirm **BACK UP + RESET BATTLE.NET BROWSER CACHE**. This *renames/moves* supported local Cache/BrowserCaches/GPUCache/Code Cache folders to timestamped backups; it must not delete Agent, game installs, or preferences. It may or may not help. Recheck CEF report.
- New diagnostics should determine next targeted GitHub fixes: CEF subprocess/GPU/sandbox, inter-process Agent communication, cache/config, or bundled Wine/Box64 edge cases. **Do not assert any hypothesis is proven.**
- **Diablo IV / DX12 compatibility remains unverified.**

## 5. Architecture and code map

The application is **Charoite Portal** (Android frontend) plus a common **Charoite Core** (runtime/services). It still derives from pinned open-source Winlator foundation `brunodev85/winlator-app` SHA `8077f374ef2d54127b088de9e8cd7b4af14d9c8b` with Wine/Box64, XServer and Android graphics/input integration. Do **not** misrepresent this as an independent replacement for Winlator/Wine. No upstream-branded user-facing menu should leak; retain license notices.

- `runtime-preview/TouhouEntryActivity.java` — native Portal shell; Library, Setup, System; Battle.net UI and troubleshooting actions.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteCore.java` — shared engine prep/compatibility policies.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteGameSessions.java` — launch intent and active-environment snapshots; handheld vs installer vs DeX.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteEnvironments.java` — separate environment registry, Wine container IDs, stable per-realm data paths; never confuse a different Activity's preferences with Portal's.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteBattleNetSupport.java` — official installer/client discovery, diagnostics, cache backup.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/diagnostics/charoite_bnet_network_probe.c` — same-session Win32 monitor/network checks.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteThemes.java` — original Charoite cosmic/Touhou sakura theme artwork.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteTpdpTouchControls.java` — on-screen handheld buttons and popup keyboard.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteWindowPolicy.java` — game/window scaling, orientation policy.
- `runtime-preview/charoite-core/CharoiteLegacyKeyboard.java` — crucial Touhou 8 scan-code bridge.
- `runtime-preview/overlay.py` — programmatic overlay onto pinned upstream Java/Android source. **Python strings generating Java require correct double escaping**, especially Windows paths; a previous CI failure was caused by illegal Java escapes.
- `.github/workflows/runtime-preview.yml` — complete signed APK build, native utilities, tests and packaging.
- `runtime-preview/tests/` — Python contract tests, including Battle.net preservation rules and new session drawer tests.

## 6. Product behavior and features to preserve

**Environments:** All environments share Core but have independent Wine C: drives/containers, libraries, theme/wallpaper, settings and isolated data. Built-ins: General Gaming, Touhou Realm, Battle.net Gaming. System supports switch/create/install/export `.cenv` settings profiles; these **do not** include full games or saves. Keep generated `C:\\CharoitePortal\\Environments\\<id>\\` data stable. Preserve legacy installations and user choices, don't overwrite selected environment.

**Library & Setup:** Library should only show explicitly consented game shortcuts. Removing a shortcut **does not uninstall game data**; users can re-add without reinstall. Show official icons when available. Selecting an installer folder **does not run arbitrary EXEs**; allow manual selection when multiple setup EXEs exist and launch one window. Actual removal of game files must be deliberate and confirmed.

**Handheld controls:** Transparent purple Game Boy-style shell; reliable equal-size D-pad and ABXY, Start/Select/shoulder and popup keyboard. Correct game-specific scan-code keyboard profiles. Touhou 8 mapping has been repaired; do not regress. Viewport must remain correctly scaled/framed, not drawn beyond bezel; support portrait/landscape and Galaxy tall displays. Hide Keys must also hide keyboard button. DeX/windows installers must not get a handheld overlay.

**Japanese/Touhou:** TPDP base and Shard of Dreams were made playable after DXVK+Turnip, Win32 CP932/locale font handling, graphics/window fixes. Existing Japanese installer rendering and Shard expansion paths need preservation. Game-specific compatibility should live in Core when reasonable, not require developer diagnostics toggled by end users. Don't bundle proprietary games or patches.

**Safety/labels:** No visible Winlator frontend; license attribution remains. Preserve package `com.charoite`, Wine saves/prefixes and settings. Preserve installable APK signing identity. Do not claim Battle.net/Diablo IV/any unverified title runs successfully because CI passed.

Keep it concise, remove superseded *current-state* statements, preserve historically significant evidence.

Existing browser cache backup/reset remains available as a reversible user-approved check, but the larger issue may require engine updates. ## 8. October 27, 2026 — Windows 11 compatibility / next Battle.net engineering plan (research only)

**User question:** "What's next for battle.net? Can we make the environment suitable for windows 11 applications and games? Would that require modifications to wine?"

**Answer and scope:** Yes, Charoite Core can aim for modern Windows 10/11 *application compatibility*, but it will not run an actual copy of Windows 11. A Windows 11 OS-version profile alone does not implement missing Win32 APIs, DirectX 12, DRM, anti-cheat/kernel drivers or GPU features. **No runtime implementation was requested explicitly or committed in this discussion; this section records verified research and proposed engineering stages only.**

**Verified source inspection (GitHub live `main` and pinned upstream):**
- Pinned Winlator-derived source: `brunodev85/winlator-app` at SHA `8077f374ef2d54127b088de9e8cd7b4af14d9c8b`. Its `WineInfo.MAIN_WINE_VERSION` is **Wine 10.10 custom**; `DefaultVersion.BOX64 = "0.4.4"`; `DXVK = 2.4.1` (plus legacy 1.10.3); `VKD3D = 2.14.1` is **defined**, not verified as successfully running DirectX 12 on the device. Turnip, Vortek, VirGL and Zink are among included graphics choices.
- The upstream Wine **10.10** source `programs/winecfg/appdefaults.c` already contains `win11` with Windows 11 reported version/build (10.0, 22000), plus `win10` default. Thus **do not patch or fork Wine just to claim a Windows 11 version selection**. Confirm the actual bundled custom build recognizes `win11` at runtime before promising a UI toggle. Official Wine 11.0 was released Jan. 13, 2026 and contains substantial WoW64 and other compatibility improvements, but is **not bundled** by this pinned baseline.
- Pinned upstream `DXWrappers` defines `VKD3D`, but `parseIdentifier()` only accepts `WINED3D` or `DXVK`; thus a modern DX12 stack/actual VKD3D selection **cannot be assumed** from the constant. Prove that compatible D3D12 DLL/DXGI and Vulkan 1.3/features are installed/configured and working rather than presenting a misleading label.
- Official Blizzard Diablo IV Windows minimum is **Windows 10 1909+**, **DirectX 12**, SSD and 90 GB space, 8 GB RAM minimum; setting Windows 11 is **not required** for its published base OS requirement. Charoite Android/Box64/GPU compatibility and game sign-in/anti-tamper remain **unverified**.
- Wine version/profile changes can alter Wine prefix state. **Do not automatically upgrade the existing Battle.net or Touhou Wine containers** to Wine 11, change package ID or replace DXVK/VKD3D. Test separately with explicit backups and rollback.

## 11. October 27, 2026 — Possible future Google Play commercial release / ads

**User asked:** once fully working, can Charoite Portal be monetized as a paid Google Play app or free with advertising? **Answer: potentially yes, but only after compliance and distribution work; neither approval nor profitability is assured.** Do not implement ad SDK or paid features until user decides the model.

**Licenses:** pinned `brunodev85/winlator-app` base has LGPL-2.1 source; commercial distribution is permitted if obligations are satisfied (source availability, notices, modified portions/relinking where required). Audit **all components** (Wine, Box64, Mesa, DXVK, VKD3D, fonts, art, any proprietary DLL dependencies) individually. Current project bundles upstream notice/license + source archive in Actions, but that alone is **not a legal compliance certification**. Do not imply a proprietary exclusive right over upstream code. Avoid shipping unlicensed Blizzard/Touhou/Diablo executables, assets, logos or misleading trademark/official-association claims; let users provide games they own.

**Key verified blockers in current source (do not confuse old `app/` Kotlin variant with actual Wine runtime build):**
- Actual runtime built from pinned upstream `app/build.gradle` has **`targetSdkVersion 28`** and `compileSdk 35` (overlay only changes `applicationId` and signing, not target). As of **Aug 31, 2026 Google Play new Android apps/updates require target API 36** (possibly eligible extension through Nov 1); migrating a Wine/Box64 app from target 28 to 36 needs deep Android storage, native exec, 16KB pages, system and foreground-service behavior audits. This is a substantial release engineering project.
- GitHub workflow currently builds signed **`assembleDebug` APK**, not Google Play-ready **signed Android App Bundle (AAB)**. Must add separate Play production flavor/process, increment versionCode, secure production signing/Play App Signing, and test update/migration from existing debug signature without loss of `com.charoite` private Wine data.
- Bundle compressed artifact approximately **231 MB**. Google currently lists **500MB base module** Play size cap, with >200MB mobile-data notice; verify true per-device AAB compressed size in Play Console and optimize if needed. See https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9859372
- Google's device/network-abuse policy forbids app self-updates outside Play and limits downloading Android executable code from elsewhere, with a **VM/interpreter exception**. Charoite downloads Windows `.exe` inside Wine: must evaluate actual architecture and policy (and third-party network terms) rather than assume exception or approval. https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/16559646
- Google Play IP policy forbids infringement and confusing trademarks: https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9888072
- Monetization options include paid Play listing or free with tasteful Portal-only banner/native ads and optional paid ad-removal via compliant Play Billing. **Do not put ads over gameplay, installers, login, controller overlays or sessions.** Respect Play ads policy, clearly disclose ads, provide privacy policy/Data Safety/consent as applicable. https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9857753 and https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/10787469
- Play developer registration currently **US$25 one time**. New *personal* developer accounts created after Nov 13 2023 generally need **12 continuously opted-in closed testers for 14 days** before applying for production access. https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/6112435/ and https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/14151465

**Recommended future roadmap:** solve core Battle.net/game UX first, document real hardware/device matrix, audit LGPL/other licenses and IP, modernize runtime for API 36, build a production AAB and signing migration, complete privacy and Play review/closed testing, then choose pricing or ads. Do not claim store-ready now. Keep the existing downloadable APK development workflow separate from eventual Play distribution.

**Evidence motivating targeted test:** Wine's own `rpcrt4/rpc_epmap.c` implements service activation through `OpenServiceW(..., SERVICE_START | SERVICE_QUERY_STATUS)` and `StartServiceW`. The on-device stopped status justifies testing explicit activation, but not a blanket system rewrite. Source: https://github.com/wine-mirror/wine/blob/master/dlls/rpcrt4/rpc_epmap.c

Never change service startup mode, registry, installed Blizzard files, game files or any save. Note the helper is dispatched via `WinHandler.exec` **after Wine's display bridge is ready**; it is not a pre-executable startup hook. ## 13. October 27, 2026 — RpcSs StartService error 1058; temporary startup-type repair APK built

**New real Galaxy report supplied by user (17:17 EDT):** They selected **TRY BATTLE.NET (START WINE RPC SERVICE)** in run `40584206916`. Confirmed `monitor_rpc_opt_in_start_requested=1`, `monitor_rpc_start_before_state=1` (STOPPED), `monitor_rpc_start_attempted=1`, and **`monitor_rpc_start_win32_error=1058`**. Wine service remained STOPPED in 16/16 samples; SCM connect successful in 16/16 with zero query/open errors. Battle.net client present 16/16 samples (max 5 processes); Agent present 15/16. Saved browser hardware acceleration OFF. Six logs: 118 error-like lines, 52 rendering refs, 16 GPU/compositor refs, 13 GPU-process refs, 3 CEF refs, 3 Agent/IPC refs, 6 network refs, 3 login/auth refs. No confirmed login success; never assert error counts themselves identify a GPU fault.

**Interpretation:** Microsoft identifies Win32 **1058** as `ERROR_SERVICE_DISABLED`: the service cannot start because it is disabled or there are no enabled associated devices. This makes original STARTUP TYPE inspection the next logical test; a blind retry of `StartServiceW` alone would be ineffective. Source: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/troubleshoot/windows-client/performance/error-1058-displayed-when-service-stops . The Wine RpcSs role is documented in `wine-mirror/wine:dlls/rpcrt4/rpc_epmap.c`. The exact reason Wine's custom Battle.net prefix has it stopped/disabled remains to be established. User has not verified the GPU-process trial resolves spinner.

**New implementation on existing `main`:**
- `2aa33c527f76122c1c99ec1ab42ba93ca526e7c8`: extend read-only same-session Wine SCM probe `charoite_bnet_network_probe.c` with `QueryServiceConfigW` start type and bounded config errors; report per-sample disabled/manual counts, last start type, and Win32 query errors. Normal non-opt-in monitoring remains read-only.
- `8ea1f5ef0016c919b2679934f0e028104d24181b`: the already-explicit Battle.net-only `/monitor /try-rpcss-start` mode now **only if start type is exactly `SERVICE_DISABLED`**: save a fixed-format marker `C:\\CharoitePortal\\Diagnostics\\charoite_rpcss_recovery.txt` in the active Wine container and fsync it; temporarily call `ChangeServiceConfigW` to `SERVICE_DEMAND_START`; call `StartServiceW`, poll bounded status, then call `ChangeServiceConfigW` back to original `SERVICE_DISABLED`; query and confirm rollback, then remove marker. Failed or interrupted restore retains marker, and a **future explicit opt-in attempt first tries to restore Disabled safely** before another attempt. Never modify other realms, Agent, games, or service startup policy permanently as a feature. A crash between change/restore can leave startup type Manual until opt-in recovery; report clearly and do not conceal risk. `aa29752deebe5708c6d823384107e701f450c0a2` completes necessary C string header for native compilation.
- `196200df49b9be2313b776c5b5dba5b5621d429c`: revises Battle.net Setup explanatory text and confirmation: this experiment **temporarily changes service startup** and attempts restoration, warns about interrupted rollback and instructs to close prior client sessions and keep it open ~30 s. Button remains **TRY BATTLE.NET (START WINE RPC SERVICE)**. Existing launch scoping in Core and monitor wiring preserved.
- `b65dd6ad6b74754d0cdaa38f84fcdfa36cc70106`: report now explains `monitor_rpc_config_disabled_samples` / start type, temporary-enable outcome, and **confirmed vs unconfirmed rollback**, with exact errors.
- `43af440e6294aa2a11fee249ba01086579548d50`, `15549330124067cb0f8c46b24e0e9ca0702bf18b`, `ca0cc5ebef33434d223e71a1dfd219a5a9027ff6`, `fc30b98b033ef67a1ba7e7a8d0f430e275be0117`: update contract tests for read-only /monitor versus opt-in-only service changes, recovery marker and UI consent. - The Charoite hook `startCharoiteBattleNetMonitor()` is triggered from `onMapWindow` when a renderable window first opens, and only after WinHandler keyboard bridge becomes ready does it run the Win32 helper via `winHandler.exec(..., "/monitor /try-rpcss-start")`. **Thus RpcSs is started after initial client launch, not before the first CEF initialization.** The observed service availability could be too late for an earlier failed CEF login bootstrap. This is a **plausible timing hypothesis, not yet proven**.
- The opt-in temporary change already restored original service start type correctly, so don't change settings persistently or undo working fix without new evidence.

If yes, proceed to Blizzard login interaction and then Diablo III installation testing, carefully protecting credentials and legal game ownership. If it still spins, prioritize a **pre-client-start** scoped RPC bootstrap mechanism (or a controlled one-session CEF restart after RpcSs is running) *before* further broad Wine version or registry changes. Ensure rollback is robust on process failure, cancel, and screen rotation. Then investigate CEF GPU/log categories and Agent IPC. Keep CI and APK verification and living handoff updated.

## 18. October 27, 2026 — Historical Agent port 1120 refused; new owner-scoped TCP listener diagnostic built

**User's actual 22:41 EDT Galaxy findings using APK run `40621810208`:**
```
preflight_rpc_ready=1
preflight_client_spawn_error=0
preflight_launch_ready=1
monitor_rpc_start_after_state=4

monitor_rpc_service_running_samples=16

monitor_client_child_present_samples=13
monitor_agent_loopback_1120_tests=4
monitor_agent_loopback_1120_connect_success=0
monitor_agent_loopback_1120_last_winsock_error=10061
```
WinSock `10061` is **connection refused** at `127.0.0.1:1120`. It does NOT prove that the Agent IPC transport fails: the port was only historically documented and may be obsolete. `Agent.exe` lives in the Wine session; four Battle.net child processes were present, but process ancestry alone cannot identify which are Chromium browser/GPU helpers. One browser config reports acceleration OFF. Logs: 6 files (2 modified since preflight), 62 error-like lines, 20 rendering refs, 8 GPU/compositor, 5 GPU-process, 3 CEF, 3 Agent/IPC, 6 network, 3 login/auth; zero narrow CEF subprocess exits, empty login URL, certificate errors, or counted crashes. Counts may overlap or include older content. User has not expressly confirmed UI outcome for this *latest* launch; earlier reported spinner.

**Public technical verification:** Microsoft's `GetExtendedTcpTable` supports `TCP_TABLE_OWNER_PID_LISTENER` for IPv4, and the pinned Wine 10.10 source contains the implementation. This is a read-only way to discover listener ownership without guessing a port. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/iphlpapi/nf-iphlpapi-getextendedtcptable . `lutris/docs/Battle.Net.md` describes spinning login symptoms and optionally resetting browser caches after acceleration is disabled; do not delete Blizzard data automatically. https://github.com/lutris/docs/blob/master/Battle.Net.md

**Real GitHub `main` changes:**
- `c806cdc65a3f9f63e5e47b4be9514e50c184a6a4` updates `runtime-preview/charoite-core/diagnostics/charoite_bnet_network_probe.c`. Keeps *in-memory only* up to 16 PIDs for `Agent.exe` observed through ToolHelp; on the 4 existing staggered samples calls `GetExtendedTcpTable` `TCP_TABLE_OWNER_PID_LISTENER` (IPv4) with a bounded 512 KiB table, counts only `LISTEN` rows with PID matching Agent and counts loopback/wildcard owned listeners. Outputs `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_owner_queries`, `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_owner_query_success`, `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_listener_present_samples`, `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_max_listeners`, `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_max_loopback_listeners`, `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_example_loopback_port`, and `monitor_agent_tcp_ipv4_last_query_error`. No port scanning, arbitrary process information, IP addresses, commands, credentials or packet data, and no new connects to discovered ports. **If count=0 or error, may reflect Wine API limitations, named pipes, Unix sockets or IPv6—not proof of Agent failure.**
- `647e2390727e9763650539f050c7d6d482bde8ef` links `-liphlpapi` in `.github/workflows/runtime-preview.yml` for 32-bit native helper.
- `5a30f2f64ac0f382696eb8424b8681262ef47858` extends the read-only `CharoiteBattleNetSupport.inspectInSessionAgentMonitor()` output, including a clear warning against over-interpreting missing IPv4 owned listeners.
- `6ec4310caa2b35eccdaf094e465121450e70bdf5` strengthens `runtime-preview/tests/test_battlenet_cef_agent_ipc_contract.py` for owner-PID matching, privacy, bounded queries, linking and report wording.

README says users install and **sign in through actual Battle.net**, and documents a **Samsung Galaxy S26 Ultra** in tested devices. `docs/TESTED_DEVICES.md` specifies Galaxy S26 Ultra, SM-S948U, Android 16/One UI 8.5, logged in and reached WoW Classic Era **in-world gameplay** after manual completion by its account owner (Oct 16). See https://github.com/ashmerra/wehavephones/blob/main/docs/TESTED_DEVICES.md and https://github.com/ashmerra/wehavephones/issues/18 . This establishes a concrete example that hardware alone does NOT prohibit Battle.net login on the target phone. It does not establish that the user personally runs that app, that Charoite's current Wine supports it, that this result generalizes to all versions/devices, or that Diablo IV works.

**Documented working runtime** in https://github.com/ashmerra/wehavephones/blob/main/docs/WEHAVEPHONES_RUNTIME.md :
- `proton-10.0-34-arm64ec` (GE-Proton 10.0-34), patched Wine `ntdll.dll`/`ntdll.so` and `wineserver`; project also uses FEX execution infrastructure.
- `--disable-gpu --enable-features=NetworkServiceInProcess2` Chromium flags, `FEX_MULTIBLOCK=0`, `MESA_SHADER_CACHE_DISABLE=false`, `WINE_FDSAN_LEVEL=off`; per-device graphics configuration (including Turnip options).
- Project reports Proton 10 rendered Battle.net CEF, whereas their tested GE-Proton 11 variants did NOT (CEF browser/renderer Mojo connection timeout), demonstrating that blindly upgrading Wine/Proton can regress login. This is a **project-specific reported result**, not universal proof about all Proton 11.
- Their Build Assets docs describe pinned downloaded runtime assets and distinct runtime setup; this is a larger architectural change than extra command-line switches, not safe to transplant unreviewed into Charoite. Respect original attribution/licensing and pinned asset hashes.
- Charoite currently uses pinned upstream Winlator-derived Wine/Box64 runtime with per-environment Wine containers (see repo README and `.github/workflows/runtime-preview.yml`). **Proposed (NOT IMPLEMENTED):** an OPTIONAL separate Battle.net-compatible engine selection for Battle.net Gaming, using an isolated new prefix and never overwriting Touhou/TPDP existing runtime or user installation. Review architecture and possible dual-runtime asset footprint/licensing, then implement incremental authenticated pinned downloads/builds and compare same phone results before migrating/retiring any engine.
- Latest verified Charoite APK is GitHub Actions `40641148611` (code SHA `553d9cbd8dc49edc84016fda2adc83cb27c031b5`, 138 tests passed, artifact `12795777267`). **No APK was built in this feasibility-review turn.** A new runtime is NOT installed.
- **Diablo IV** needs separate compatibility/performance tests (64-bit Windows game, DirectX 12, substantial resources), and WoW success cannot be extrapolated to Diablo IV.

**Message to user:** This is technically possible, backed by an independently documented result even on Galaxy S26 Ultra, but not guaranteed to work with current Charoite engine. Acknowledge excessive prior incremental diagnostics without a working UI. Recommend runtime-level comparison with the functioning implementation, not further repetitive port/RPC testing. No uninstall or clearing existing data.

Series now contains 6 active Charoite patches (+70 upstream prereqs, +10 GE). Preparation and tests enforce counts.

The earlier run [40739727774](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/40739727774) stopped at a source-provenance test because README did not yet mention patch 0500; adding its explicit attribution and scope fixed that test.

Both DLLs link successfully with the targeted Clang/Wine `libgcc.a` correction. Kernel artifact metadata sets `launch_ready=false`, `installable=false`, and requires the independently built base slice.

## 36. October 28, 2026 — Correcting Android clipboard Winsock compile regression in new ARM64EC user32 DLL

**New foundation CI diagnosis:** [run 40786056442](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/40786056442) on c86b9154b020082e9e320168e5bfd50961d53da7 failed only in the new PE foundation build after source, host, Android wineserver, ntdll, kernel and wineboot stages had passed. Implementation, **Next ONLY**:
- `next-generation/CharoiteNextBootActivity.java` is the launcher-cold-start activity. - `next-generation/generate_crystal_startup.py` generates a **new, original** non-copyrighted approximately **1.85-second** crystalline glass chime (44.1-kHz mono 16-bit PCM WAV) using four ascending harmonics and gentle reflections; automatically compiled as `res/raw/charoite_crystal_startup.wav`. No online audio or unknown binary was imported.

## 51. October 29, 2026 — Next v0.8 real warm-app reentry lifecycle fix (device validation pending)

**User-provided Galaxy S26 Ultra observation:** Next v0.7 5-second crystal boot displays when APK is first updated/cold launched, but **does not recur on subsequent app openings** when Android resumes an already-running app. This is a confirmed functional UX defect despite v0.7 compilation success.

**Root cause:** Next v0.7 `CharoiteNextBootActivity` only set `REENTRY_NEEDED` in `noteAppVisibility` on `ComponentCallbacks2.TRIM_MEMORY_UI_HIDDEN`. Android **does not guarantee** this callback whenever the user taps Home and later resumes the app. A resumed task can bring `TouhouEntryActivity` to foreground without recreating `CharoiteNextBootActivity`.

**Direct GitHub `main` source fix for v0.8**:
- `next-generation/CharoiteNextBootActivity.java`: `installReentryTracker` registers `Application.ActivityLifecycleCallbacks` **once per process** from the Portal creation; it tracks started Charoite activities, records when the last started activity stops without configuration change, and marks boot splash pending when **Portal itself** later starts from the background. Internal Portal→game transitions are intended to retain a started app activity and not repeat the boot. The existing five-second full-screen modal replay and crystal one-shot sound are retained; active modal dismissed if Portal stops. `noteLauncherIntent` additionally handles explicit Android `ACTION_MAIN`+`CATEGORY_LAUNCHER` reentry (e.g. DeX warm relaunch).
- `next-generation/prepare_next.py`: installs tracker in Portal `onCreate`; uses existing `onResume` to display pending boot, removes unreliable `onTrimMemory` UI-hidden wiring. Adds `onNewIntent` for explicit launcher warm intent, deferring UI display with `decorView.post` to avoid dialog before window readiness. **Only Charoite Next package `com.charoitx`, version `11.2-next.0.8`, versionCode `39`**; original stable `com.charoite` not modified.
- `next-generation/tests/test_startup_experience.py` now expects Android ActivityLifecycleCallbacks, multi-activity and configuration isolation and rejects prior trim-memory dependence. Native-wine test/version contracts updated; `CharoiteNextRuntime` status updated.
- `.github/workflows/charoite-next.yml` validates v0.8 identity; keeps exact 768px boot art, 5-second progress, crystal audio, same signing key and audited Wine loader/ntdll/wineserver.
- Source changes: `9082825b6f5909102140de9d35bdf6089d34d70f` (lifecycle tracker); `01e08efbbdc885cf18cce77a9cacefb3d6ee568c` (Next integration); `856b16d7724373a8bd79bf97e8906858ae7a0608` (tests/workflow); `9d229e3bd531532ad84f24d54e59e36cf3588ccb` (safe onNewIntent dialog post).

**Build tracking:** Earlier run `40944991755` was **canceled** under workflow concurrency by a newer `main` push; do not use its artifacts or misreport it as a successful APK. Replacement push build commit `9d229e3bd531532ad84f24d54e59e36cf3588ccb`, live run **40945036353**: https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/40945036353 . The generated 44.1kHz mono 16-bit standard PCM WAV uses Android `AudioTrack` MODE_STATIC for dependable first-use playback, with MediaPlayer as fallback if the raw resource or AudioTrack fails. Playback respects MEDIA volume (does not force unmute); diagnostic report records QUEUED/STARTED/PASS/FAILED and mute. A warm boot plays the same audio after the five-second overlay ends. ## 60. October 29, 2026 — Charoite Next 0.15 startup-crystal sound click/static reduction (SIGNED APK VERIFIED)

**User report:** Charoite Next 0.14 appears to boot and replay correctly, but the startup crystal sound has static/crackling. The supplied 11.3-second screen recording `173619.mp4` contains **H.264 video only and NO AUDIO STREAM**, so the noise heard on device could not be directly captured or attributed to playback hardware.

**Concrete source defect diagnosed (NOT speculative):** `next-generation/generate_crystal_startup.py` previously adds a delayed reflection at 0.36 seconds after each oscillator onset without a fade-in:
`if elapsed > .36: ... amp*.105*exp(...)*sin(2*pi*hz*reflection+.72)`. Because `sin(.72)` is nonzero, the echo appears instantaneously in the PCM waveform, creating a discontinuity around 0.36, 0.60, 1.14, and 1.54 seconds. A numerical waveform probe found a first abrupt step ~0.0181 (full-scale units) at 0.36 sec; the overall peak was ~0.287, so **no full-scale digital clipping**. Abrupt echo onsets can sound like clicks or crackle; whether this exactly matches the user's perceived "static" remains unconfirmed without audio-bearing recording.

**Implemented:** Direct commit to `main` `ca9a93ff30beb1f3d3e18fce7bace01b8a075c5c`. Introduce a 140ms `smoothstep(reflection/.14)` echo attack, reduce echo amplitude (.105→.085), soften metallic detuned overtones (.82/.16/.07 → .90/.095/.025 with frequencies 2.003/3.006 multipliers). Keeps original 4.2s slow mysterious crystal feel while avoiding sudden sample transitions. **Unchanged:** Android `AudioTrack` playback/MediaPlayer fallback, boot image, boot lifecycle, Wine/native modules, game data/prefixes/saves and unrelated core systems.

**Regression:** New `next-generation/tests/test_crystal_audio_quality.py` tests actual signal's second-difference near each echo onset, finite unclipped output, and expected standard 44-byte RIFF WAV/44.1kHz/16-bit mono compatible with Android's current AudioTrack playback. Local numerical probe shows echo second differences far below the 0.004 threshold (new first ~0.00002 vs old ~0.015 jump), peak about 0.299 and tail near zero.

**Build:**
- Charoite Next **`11.2-next.0.15` versionCode 46**, package **`com.charoitx`**. Source/CI commit `91ceee278d3adb634bbc4062306a60f44d68c782`, only main branch.
- **GitHub Actions run 40977885525 — SUCCESS**, https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/40977885525, no failed steps. **26 Next regression tests passed**, plus **138 stable runtime-preview regression tests passed**. All new echo tests passed. APK original artwork and startup chime resource verified. APK identity `com.charoitx` versionCode 46 and same persistent signing certificate confirmed.
- **APK ZIP artifact ID 12943088768**, `Charoite-Core-Next-Android-APK`, https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/40977885525/artifacts/12943088768; ZIP size 237,285,563 bytes; ZIP SHA-256 `241c8817488caa860d88d2e9fd3a0356b4061696a8d2178688ff901f68d79ef9` (archive hash, NOT inner APK hash). Licenses artifact ID 12943133535.
- **Device acceptance remains untested:** install 0.15 IN PLACE (never clear data), verify boot returns and new sound is cleaner. If static continues, get audio-enabled recording, Android startup diagnostics and inspect whether AudioTrack resampling, audio focus, sound effects or actual device playback contribute. The previous supplied recording had no audio and cannot prove the precise audio defect.

## 62. October 29, 2026 — Charoite Next 0.17 restores boot on genuine launch, prevents app-switch replay (SIGNED APK PASS)

**User report:** After installing Next 0.16, the boot screen no longer came up. Previous request: boot screen must not replay when the application is already open and the user switches between apps. Keep these separate: *new actual app session* requires boot; Home/Recents/app switching of an existing Portal does not.

**Root cause in 0.16:** BootActivity launched Portal after a 5-second delay and immediately called `finish()`, destroying the launcher task root. Samsung/Android could then restore the existing Portal directly, bypassing the launcher BootActivity, and a weak-reference "live Portal" check could suppress a legitimate launcher boot. The absence of a process-aware cold-start fallback after app update compounded this regression.

**Fixes committed DIRECTLY to the existing `main` branch:**
- `next-generation/CharoiteNextBootActivity.java`: initial BootActivity **no longer finishes itself immediately after starting Portal**. It remains behind Portal as task root so Android retains a consistent launcher route. If the user actually exits Portal, BootActivity resumes with `completed=true`, finishes itself and permits a new five-second boot on next genuine launch. If an existing live Portal is in the *same task*, a redundant launcher instance routes to Portal with `FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP|FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP` rather than booting or dumping the user to Home.
- On actual `TouhouEntryActivity.onCreate` only, `onPortalCreated(this, savedInstanceState)` registers the session and detects an old/restored *direct Portal task root*, which can occur after an APK update and bypass BootActivity. When this is a genuine new activity or version-changed restore rather than a routine configuration change/internal activity, it renders `createBootView` as an unobtrusive five-second root overlay and plays the same cleaned-up crystal audio after completion. The last shown APK versionCode is recorded in internal startup diagnostics; fresh v0.17 code 48 ensures the first restored entry after upgrading from 0.16 can receive the boot fallback.
- Normal `onResume`, Home, Recents, app switching, and window focus **do not create a boot overlay**. Normal onResume only consumes the one-time startup sound marker for a real previous cold boot. The 0.16 full-bleed, transparent-corner approved artwork and purple backdrop, progress bar, and smooth sound are unchanged.
- Next-only version bump to **11.2-next.0.17, versionCode 48, `com.charoitx`**; same persistent APK signing key. Stable Charoite Portal, game data, save files, Wine environments, prefixes, and installed Windows games are not modified.
- Added regression checks that prevent immediately finishing the launcher after starting Portal, assert same-task detection and Android task-root retention, validate old-task direct Portal fallback occurs only on creation with version checking, and prevent warm splash on `onResume`. New startup diagnostics retain a copyable event trace.

**Verified GitHub Actions APK:**

## 63. October 29, 2026 — Next 0.18 fixes brief Portal flash before cold fallback boot (SIGNED APK PASS)

**User report (real Samsung Galaxy S26 Ultra):** After v0.17, the startup boot was almost correct, but the main Portal screen briefly flashed **before** the five-second boot screen on subsequent genuine launches. Requirement remains: genuine new launch/closed app → boot, existing open app switched away and back → no boot/sound replay.

**Precisely identified cause:** In `next-generation/CharoiteNextBootActivity.java`, when Samsung restores the Portal directly as task root, `onPortalCreated()` deferred the fallback with `showColdPortalFallback(portal, 0)`, which ran `getDecorView().postDelayed(..., 80ms)`. It waited for `android.R.id.content.isAttachedToWindow()`, retrying after 160ms. The Portal could render its actual main page before the delayed splash overlay arrived. The source tests in 0.17 checked eventual display, not first-frame coverage.

**Implemented directly on the EXISTING main branch:**
- `CharoiteNextBootActivity.java`: `onPortalCreated()` now marks `pendingColdFallbackOwner` only, without posting or delaying any overlay. New `onPortalPostCreated(Activity)` synchronously adds the same approved full-bleed five-second boot view to `android.R.id.content` after the Portal's `onCreate` UI assembly but **before first draw**; no attachment/focus wait, no transient main page. The overlay continues to dismiss after five seconds and plays the clean crystal audio. `notePortalDestroyed` cleans up pending fallback references. The original task-root cold boot path and no-replay-on-Home/Recents code from 0.17 are untouched.
- `next-generation/prepare_next.py`: inserts `@Override protected void onPostCreate(Bundle)` into Next's generated `TouhouEntryActivity`, calling `.onPortalPostCreated(this)` synchronously after `super.onPostCreate`; proactively rejects an unexpected upstream duplicate override. Keeps onResume for one-time cold-arrival audio only. No new files/data modifications.
- `next-generation/tests/test_startup_experience.py`: new regression asserts synchronous overlay addition **before** any five-second delayed dismissal, checks no `postDelayed`/attachment waiting for installation, ensures old delayed method gone, ensures no app-switch overlays, confirms existing five-second progress and sound. `test_native_wine_smoke.py` and Actions package checks updated to 0.18.
- Version `11.2-next.0.18`, **versionCode 49**, Android package `com.charoitx`; same persistent signing key. Approved 1536px image, transparent corners/fullbleed purple background and smooth crystal startup sound **unchanged**. Original stable Charoite Portal, Wine, prefixes, installed games and saves untouched.

## 77. October 30, 2026 — Next v0.28 seamless seven-second cloud boot and crystal audio — VERIFIED APK

**User feedback:** Next v0.27 five-second boot sound sounds abruptly cut off on Galaxy S26 Ultra; asks to make BOTH crystal-cloud animation and boot sound seamless, approximately seven seconds. This report is the first device-based observation on v0.27 sound; do not claim v0.28 is phone-verified yet.

**Root-cause assessment:** In v0.27 the splash dismissal uses a fixed timer starting in Activity creation, while the sound starts on a posted decor-view callback and AudioTrack creation/PCM resource loading happen later. Calling `stopBootSound()` on the seven/five-second splash timer could truncate a delayed-start waveform even if its last generated samples fade smoothly. The video decoder likewise begins asynchronously and could still show an intermediate cloud frame on dismissal. This is a plausible cause, not a measurement of actual device latency.

**Implemented directly on main (not instructions):**
- `next-generation/generate_crystal_startup.py`: seven-second original 44.1kHz mono PCM crystal resonance, **308,700 frames**, gradual 1.5-second fade from approximately 4.85s to true digital silence at 6.35s, leaving **650ms silent headroom** to avoid audible hard stops from delayed AudioTrack start. Existing gentle mystery/chime timbre and onset envelope retained, no copyrighted audio.
- `docs/previews/render_crystal_awakening.py`: **7s / 168 frames / 24fps / 480×920** silent H.264 CLOUD ONLY video with a soft late glow peak, cloud opacity easing away 5.55–6.85s; no image warp, zoom, lightning, or change to the approved art/plaque. Output `Charoite_Mystic_C_Cloud_Only_7s.mp4` and storyboard.
- `next-generation/CharoiteNextBootActivity.java`: `BOOT_DURATION_MS=7000L` normal launcher and Samsung direct-root cold fallback, references the new seven-second silent movie. Independently fade `CrystalCloudMovie` alpha to zero over 750ms starting 950ms ahead of dismissal (decoder-latency safety net; first decoded frame cannot restore alpha after fade starts). Cold-root splash crossfades into already-created Portal UI over final 650ms; normal launcher continues its existing Android fade-in/out transition. Stop sound on splash dismissal only after intended audio is already silent, and no repeat on app switching/Portal arrival.
- `next-generation/prepare_next.py`: generate exact seven-second WAV/movie, check 168-frame video and persist unchanged source art, bump Next to **`11.2-next.0.28`**, Android **versionCode 59**, package/signature **`com.charoitx`**.
- `next-generation/tests/test_crystal_audio_quality.py`, `tests/test_startup_experience.py`, `tests/test_native_wine_smoke.py`, and `.github/workflows/charoite-next.yml`: validate final 650ms of silence and fade, splash/video finish behavior, 308,700-sample PCM, seven-second 168-frame video, signed version identity and original splash/data preservation.
- `.github/workflows/charoite-preview-video.yml`: render and upload seven-second silent video-only preview.
- Atomic staged visual/audio code commit `901527a31defb837f86692c9ef93add1252b2bc4` (`[skip ci]`); final code/tests/workflow commit **`d80a86492339f36567285c2d3e8835e1eddc286c`** triggers release build.

## 98. October 30, 2026 — Wine26 loader re-exec failure and fresh v0.42 diagnostic

**On-device report confirmed (Next v0.41):** Galaxy S26 Ultra passed 99/99 (23 PE + 76 NLS) staged source SHA checks and all three native Wine26 hash/version gates; 22 ARM64EC DLL + ARM64 wineboot.exe + 76 NLS files SHA-256 copied into a brand-new one-shot private scratch. Wineboot was actually attempted, exited **1** before 25 seconds, and logged **`wine: could not exec the wine loader`**. No new private prefix directory, no system.reg. Isolated wineserver stop was requested. Existing Wine22/Portal data/games/saves/prefixes/rootfs untouched; failed v1 scratch **retained and never reused**. This is not a Wineboot pass.

**Source diagnosis (hypothesis, not established device fix):** Wine10 ntdll Unix `__wine_main` attempts a second loader exec in `pre_exec()` when `WINELOADERNOEXEC` is absent, and prints this exact message on loader reexec failure. Source: https://sources.debian.org/src/wine/10.0~repack-12/dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c . The source-built APK-owned loader has a renamed native-library filename, not the usual Wine bin/wine installation layout. This is a plausible cause, but further runtime failures could follow and 20 sampled unbundled PE import names remain.

**Targeted experimental v0.42 implemented in GitHub/main:** Code SHA `83c3c22a3c98341ed1ec6165bffdccd05610273e` adds `WINELOADERNOEXEC=1` only to the user-consented Wine26PrefixProbe's clean process environment; no alteration to source-built native Wine binaries and no claim that skipping the preloader is a generalized runtime fix. The scratch is now **`charoite-next-wine26-first-prefix-v2`**; v1 is untouched, and v2 is strictly one-shot. Retains 99+3 hash gates, isolated WINEPREFIX/HOME/TMP, source-matched loader and wineserver, 25-second timeout and bounded report. Version `11.2-next.0.42`, versionCode `73`, package `com.charoitx`, original Next signing identity/boot and stable Portal unaffected. Updated 9 regression test files and new test assertions for v2 isolation and diagnostic environment.

**Build status as recorded:** [v0.42 Actions run 41128003255](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41128003255) triggered from SHA `83c3c22a3c98341ed1ec6165bffdccd05610273e`, **in progress** when first checked; do not yet claim a signed v0.42 APK. ## 100. October 30, 2026 — Galaxy Wine26 v0.42 reaches fresh prefix creation; native NLS lookup source patch

**DEVICE ACTUAL (user supplied, Galaxy S26 Ultra, signed Charoite Next v0.42):** 99 PE/NLS staged hashes PASS, all 3 native Wine26 SHA/version probes PASS, 22 ARM64EC DLL + ARM64 wineboot.exe + 76 NLS scratch SHA-256 copy PASS. `WINELOADERNOEXEC=1` allowed Wine to continue past the earlier v0.41 native loader re-exec failure. Wineboot finished in under 25 seconds with **exit 127**; created brand-new v2 isolated prefix directory but did NOT create `system.reg`. Bound output says: `err:environ:read_nls_file failed to load .../lib/arm64/../../bin/../share/wine/nls/locale.nls` and same `l_intl.nls`; then `wine: created the configuration directory '.../charoite-next-wine26-first-prefix-v2/prefix'`. Result FAIL, not a bootable Windows prefix. v1 and v2 retained separately without clearing. Original Wine22, stable Portal, saves, rootfs and user prefixes untouched. No Windows EXE/FEX/graphics attempted.

**ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION:** Pinned original Wine source `The412Banner/proton-wine` commit `c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767`, `dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c` `init_paths()` sets `data_dir = build_relative_path(bin_dir, BINDIR, DATADIR "/wine")` after resolving `ntdll` from APK-native ELF library. In APK-native execution, that computed directory is non-writable and not the sandbox containing all 76 verified NLS tables. Wine's `read_nls_file()` reads from that internal `data_dir`, not by looking up a Java-set WINEDATADIR; therefore merely adding an environment setting in the Android app cannot be trusted as a fix. This is an upstream Wine 10 installation-layout mismatch surfaced by the v2 test.

**ACTUAL SOURCE FIX UNDER DEVELOPMENT:** Added Charoite Wine LGPL source patch `0800-android-isolated-wine-data-dir.patch` in `next-generation/charoite-wine/patches/SERIES`, appended as ninth active local patch. The patch, strictly on Android AArch64, lets `ntdll/unix/loader.c` resolve a caller-supplied absolute `CHAROITE_WINE26_DATA_DIR` via `realpath` after rudimentary path validation; no override preserves normal upstream Wine path. Added a source-preparation guard and regression for this mandatory symbol, and updated the source README and patch count. Source commit `0b1f776f15ae51c2e1ae31b6c7224ab8514437f4`. CI run `41129471534` validated all 92 Python source tests but failed on malformed unified diff header, before compilation. The hunk counts were corrected in code commit `e2bc067a6ff72e916f6dc2f74c502384e76540a6`. [Source build and validation run **41129670162**](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41129670162): **validate-wine-source job passed**; remaining host and Android native / ARM64EC jobs in progress as of this record. No new source-coherent archive result yet, and no v0.43 app APK built or tested.

**NEXT:** Confirm source CI 41129670162 completes successfully and publishes verified new 26-component archive with patched native ntdll. Retain old signed v0.42, staged Wine26 0.5 slot, and failed v1/v2 scratch. Integrate the NEW audited archive in a DIFFERENT versioned Next slot, explicitly set `CHAROITE_WINE26_DATA_DIR` only from a verified fresh app-private NLS directory, create a separate one-shot v3 prefix, retain 99 PE/NLS plus native hash gates, 25-second timeout, bounded diagnostic output, and user opt-in. No clearing or overwriting prior slots. Build/sign Next v0.43 only after pinned new Wine source artifact is verified. Do not claim the environment or games runnable without new phone test.

## 110. October 30, 2026 — v4 exit 127 with no trace; native Windows NTDLL lookup defect found in source

**User's actual v0.46 one-shot v4 device report** (no repeat authorized): all **99** staged PE/NLS source hashes PASS; three same-source native hashes and `--version` probes PASS; fresh scratch copies 22 ARM64EC DLLs + ARM64 wineboot.exe + 76 NLS SHA-256 PASS. Wineboot finished within 25 seconds, log did not exceed 8 MiB, **exit 127**, new private prefix directory exists, **`system.reg` / `user.reg` both absent**. The ENTIRE bounded log was again only:

```text
wine: created the configuration directory '/data/user/0/com.charoitx/no_backup/charoite-next-wine27-first-prefix-v4-trace/prefix'
```

The isolated v4 wineserver stop was requested. v1/v2/v3 and v4 evidence retained untouched; Wine22, Portal data, user prefixes, games, saves, rootfs not accessed. No FEX, graphics, Steam, Battle.net, or anti-cheat attempted. **Do not rerun v4 or any older used one-shot scratch.**

**Root cause candidate now backed by exact pinned Wine source:** Verified Charoite v0.45 read-only report showed all six directly imported `wineboot.exe` DLL names present. Studying pinned [The412Banner/proton-wine source `c74acb09...`](https://github.com/The412Banner/proton-wine/tree/c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767), its `dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c` initial `load_ntdll()` uses `dll_dir + get_pe_dir(current_machine) + /ntdll.dll` under APK-native ELF lookup; **that initial Windows NTDLL is NOT selected through the later `WINEDLLPATH` builtin search**. On v3/v4 the APK-native Unix `ntdll.so` resides in the APK-extracted `nativeLibraryDir`, while the hash-verified `ntdll.dll` was copied into private `runtime/lib/wine/arm64ec-windows`. This is an actual **source integration path mismatch**, but it is **not proven to be the sole cause of exit 127**. Native debug logging did not capture a failing module; avoid claiming proven success.

**Source-only patch candidate for new isolated Wine28 source cohort:** [`0900-android-isolated-windows-ntdll-pe-path.patch`](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/blob/main/next-generation/charoite-wine/patches/0900-android-isolated-windows-ntdll-pe-path.patch) added after previously verified 0800 to the 10-entry `patches/SERIES`. Android AArch64-only `CHAROITE_WINE28_PE_DIR` allows exact first Windows `ntdll.dll` to load from a SHA-verified fresh isolated scratch directory, canonical path checked, fail-closed on missing file. **Source audit of pinned The412Banner/proton-wine c74acb09:** Native `dlls/ntdll/unix/server.c` creates configuration directory and server path; `dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c` starts wineserver via `posix_spawn` and *propagates the wineserver's nonzero exit code* in `start_server`. Therefore v5 exit 127 **could** originate from a wineserver startup/exec failure before PE boot, but is NOT established. The matched server's `--version` does not exercise `open_master_socket()`. **New pinned-source insight, awaiting device validation:** In `dlls/ntdll/unix/env.c` `build_initial_params`, Wine calls `run_wineboot()` with `WINEBOOTSTRAPMODE=1` **before** launching requested PE. Its internal boot executable path is `C:\windows\system32\wineboot.exe` with `--init`. In `dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c`, ARM64 `get_pe_dir()` returns **`/aarch64-windows`**. Before prefix contents are installed, Wine's `is_builtin_path` and `find_builtin_dll` find this boot EXE under a `WINEDLLPATH` root's `aarch64-windows` leaf. v8's primary requested `wineboot.exe` was staged only in **`runtime/lib/wine/arm64-windows/wineboot.exe`**, NOT alongside the 22 DLLs in **`runtime/lib/wine/aarch64-windows`**. The subsequent current process loads kernel32 only after bootstrap and the PE loader's `find_builtin_without_file` is gated by `is_prefix_bootstrap` (Wine `dlls/ntdll/loader.c`), so missing boot can indirectly cause `kernel32 c0000135` even with kernel32's verified file present. **This is a hypothesis, not proven root cause.** `dlls/ntdll/unix/env.c` adds WINEDLLDIR* environment variables derived from `WINEDLLPATH`. `apisetschema.dll` is also unbundled; address independently, not with an invented module.

## 127. October 30, 2026 — v0.53 phone READ-ONLY v9 forensic result; release-readiness assessment

**Actual phone result from v0.53 inspector, device read-only PASS:** The preserved v9 prefix exists and contains `system.reg` and `user.reg`, but `prefix/drive_c` is absent. The existing source-verified built-in `runtime/lib/wine/aarch64-windows/wineboot.exe` and `kernel32.dll` files were confirmed present (not reread). The old Windows PE log is 7,945 bytes (56 lines), server log 23,519 bytes (250 lines); diagnostic inspected exactly these two existing files, no execution/prefix writes or new scratch. Wine's automatic `C:\\windows\\system32\\wineboot.exe --init` triggers server request `new_process(... machine=aa64 ...)`, which **returned status 0 and process pid=0028**, but the subsequent `get_new_process_info() = 0 { success=0, exit_code=1 }`: the internal child failed to initialize. The server trace found/mapped the built-in copy in v9 aarch64-windows after two unsuccessful Android APK lib lookup attempts, so **absence of the extra Wineboot PE image is no longer the leading hypothesis**. Server also reported a missing `/lib/arm64/aarch64-windows/apisetschema.dll` file (NO_SUCH_FILE); whether that is directly fatal is unproven. The outer manually requested ARM64 Wineboot PE still mapped Windows ntdll.dll, then failed `kernel32.dll c0000135` and exited 53 without showing help. Wineserver socket/lock and initial RPC work. **The precise cause of child startup failure is unknown; do not conflate server process allocation with successful Windows process initialization.** No Windows EXE or game works with this new engine yet.

**Readiness relative to 1.0:** Native Wine server and basic PE mapping are milestones, not full Windows app support. The separately maintained modern compatibility layer is **pre-alpha** and has not passed a complete clean prefix bootstrap, basic Windows program launch, FEX x86/x64 execution, display/input, DXVK/VKD3D graphics, Steam/Battle.net, compatibility/performance and safe update tests. No evidence-based completion percentage or release date can be assigned. Distinguish the more mature stable `com.charoite` Portal/Wine22 branch from the experimental `com.charoitx` modern runner. Define a bounded 1.0 supported-game/app matrix and reliable baseline first; the aspiration of an all-purpose Windows 11/Diablo IV/anti-cheat-compatible runtime is not a substantiated 1.0 commitment, and anti-cheat must not be bypassed.

**Next:** read-only pinned source/PE imports/native Android child-creation analysis of the **failed internal Wineboot child process** before adding any new diagnostic or repeating execution. Investigate child loader search, kernel32/kernelbase resolution, apiset path and additional unbundled Wine modules; distinguish missing-module hypotheses from confirmed errors. Any future experiment must use a NEW versioned scratch and user opt-in, keep v1-v9 and user data untouched, and have bounded logs/teardown. Do NOT rerun v9. **v0.53 phone read-only forensic path was successful; no new APK/code changes in this handoff-only update.** Latest signed v0.53 remains Actions 41156385893, code commit d9e7e1fb8e7b93be6c2c0f86b3893fd1cac3f016, artifact 13025014821; a docs-only [skip ci] commit should not be mistaken for a new built APK.

## 128. October 30, 2026 — automated diagnostic relay proposal (DESIGN ONLY, no telemetry enabled)

**User goal:** End the repeated manual cycle of downloading a new Charoite Next APK, copying the device's large diagnostic logs and pasting them into ChatGPT. User asked whether the process could be *fully automated*. **Answer: most of it can be automated through an explicitly opted-in Android diagnostic relay and a connected processing/development workflow, but not by a normal chat operating autonomously after the conversation ends.** No outbound diagnostic reporting, external service, permissions, credentials, automatic Wine execution, or app update installer has been implemented or authorized by this discussion.

**Proposed first milestone — reliable no-copy reporting:** Build an optional **Charoite Next diagnostics relay** in `com.charoitx` that, after the user deliberately enrolls/pairs the device, queues newly produced redacted diagnostic reports for delivery using Android-supported reliable background work (WorkManager or equivalent), HTTPS, retries/offline queue, per-report status, payload size cap, timestamps, report schema/version, and easy disable/delete/export controls. **User explicitly confirmed the GitHub repository must remain PUBLIC, and is comfortable with phone diagnostic reports being visible.** A secure authenticated intake can validate/strip sensitive content before optionally posting **sanitized** diagnostics to public GitHub issues/discussions or a dedicated report location. **Do not embed GitHub PATs, GitHub App private keys or backend service-role credentials in the APK**. Validate device identity, authenticate uploads, limit requests, restrict source fields, redact credentials/tokens/personal data/private paths; avoid raw logs, profile records, game saves and private registry data in public GitHub. Never send real customer game saves, registry/profile details, tokens, login logs, or unredacted private paths. User controls which report types are shared and can see what will be sent. Preserve existing scratch and user data.

**GitHub Actions / agent integration:** The authenticated relay can create public GitHub issue entries containing **sanitized, opted-in** structured phone diagnostics or forward an opaque report ID via a server-authenticated GitHub `repository_dispatch`; Actions run deterministic classification/unit/integrity checks and publish status and new artifacts. Never publicly expose raw device traces or credential-bearing workflow payloads. A separate configured persistent development agent/Work workflow would be required to analyze failures, modify source, wait for CI, and continue independently; GitHub Actions alone does not create an autonomous AI engineer and a normal ChatGPT conversation is not an always-running background agent. New code should be reviewed/verified; normal build signer/package integrity checks remain mandatory. Keep latest code commit vs handoff-only [skip ci] HEAD distinct.

**Device safety and installer boundary:** First implement report upload only. Optional self-tests require explicit user opt-in to *defined bounded, nondestructive test classes*. Treat each scratch test as one shot; do not automatically rerun v1-v9 or any failed scratch. New Wine execution requires a fresh safe generation, timeouts, maximum resource use, readable scope/consent, and preserved logs. APK delivery may support app notifications/download and Android PackageInstaller sessions but Android can require user approval to complete an update depending on installer identity and platform requirements; do not promise silently replacing installed `com.charoitx`. No removal/clear of apps, stable `com.charoite`, Wine22/rootfs, user prefixes, installed games/saves/wallpapers/shortcuts. Preserve signature, package identity and update continuity.

**Suggested phased implementation:** (1) define JSON report schema, sensitive-field scrubber, authenticated relay to the PUBLIC GitHub project, and opt-in UX; (2) automatic submission/retries and status receipt, test on phone with innocuous diagnostics; (3) Actions classification/notifications and artifact/version check; (4) limited authorized one-shot experiments and update workflow; (5) *optional* persistent agent with separate user-approved permissions and safeguards. User has confirmed public GitHub visibility and diagnostic-report acceptability. Still obtain opt-in account/device pairing, scope and safe credential design before enabling automatic live uploads; no GitHub visibility change is required. No developer or phone action in this section.

## 130. October 30, 2026 — autonomy goal and practical limits

**Latest user clarification:** The desired reason for automatic phone diagnostics, GitHub reporting and an ongoing development system is to **speed the Charoite Core Next path to 1.0 with as little user involvement as possible**. Aim to eliminate manual log copy/paste, repetitive build checks and manually reporting every failed experiment. Repository stays **PUBLIC**, phone diagnostics are acceptable with sanitization; do not assume public publishing authorizes leaking secrets or personal data.

**Concrete architecture:** Add an opted-in Next Android diagnostic collector with a schema, validation/redaction, bounded persistent offline/retry queue and an authenticated upload relay. Store or publish only sanitized diagnostic summaries, using public GitHub issues or Actions-compatible report triggers when appropriate. Avoid embedding credentials in the APK. GitHub Actions already builds, runs tests, checks signing and uploads APK artifacts on new source changes, but has **no attached phone test runner**, no current automatic reporting from Android, no persistent autonomous developer, and no unattended Android APK installer. Add an explicit backend and a **separately configured persistent development agent** to consume report IDs, analyze failures and propose/commit fixes, trigger CI and evaluate results. Build a phone-side verified update notification/check and controlled device-test mechanism. Never claim a standard ChatGPT chat continues working after the session ends. GitHub Actions cannot prove on-device Wine runtime functionality by itself.

**Phasing:** (1) Automatic device diagnostic reporting with visible upload/off switch; (2) authenticated intake, structured failure indexing and CI notification; (3) persistent developer workflow capable of fixing code with tests/review/rollback; (4) secure update delivery and new opt-in bounded test generations; (5) measured closed-loop iterations and a realistic supported-app 1.0 test matrix. Require user involvement for initial enrollment/credentials and privacy review, occasional new destructive-risk test approvals, Android installation/update approvals when required, and exceptional device issues. Do **not** turn on arbitrary remote code execution, unattended destructive tests, auto-delete old scratch or auto-change stable Wine22/Portal prefixes, games, saves and rootfs. Leave v1-v9 evidence unchanged. No automation is currently implemented or enabled; this response is a plan only.

**Next implementation task:** Design and implement the optional phone diagnostic reporting path first, then connect it to the PUBLIC GitHub repository through a safe authenticated relay. Keep v0.53 as the currently verified signed Next baseline; v9's child `get_new_process_info success=0 exit_code=1` and `kernel32 c0000135` remain unresolved.

## 134. October 30, 2026 — v0.56 AndroidKeyStore AES-GCM IV and setup dialog contrast fix; CI VERIFIED

**Observed phone screenshot after v0.55 install:** `Could not enable: InvalidAlgorithmParameterException` when enabling public GitHub phone reporting. Token was masked in screenshot, and no token value should ever be requested or logged. The dialog background was nearly WHITE, with WHITE title/instructions (invisible).

**Source-confirmed root cause:** `next-generation/CharoiteNextAutoReports.java` created a 12-byte IV with `new SecureRandom().nextBytes(iv)` and passed it into `c.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key(), new GCMParameterSpec(128, iv))`, despite the key's `setRandomizedEncryptionRequired(true)`. Android Keystore rejects externally supplied IVs for randomized AES-GCM encryption and throws `InvalidAlgorithmParameterException`. On v0.55 the exception happened inside `protect()` before storing the token or enabling uploads; the user need NOT generate another token. Changed encryption to `c.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key())` then `byte[] encrypted=c.doFinal(...)` and `iv=c.getIV()`, validating IV length 12, while decryption remains `c.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key(), new GCMParameterSpec(128, iv))`. Existing Android Keystore alias preserved. No app data reset, token exposure or Wine operation. Setup panel and dialog now explicitly dark purple with white text and light-gray hint for contrast. Added static regression contract asserting Android-generated IV and readable dark dialog. These changes don't enable diagnostic uploads or execute any Wine test.

**Source commits:** `945e2669a4b63a9f24ae9dcb7f360f41428c5e1b` fix and v0.56/87 version bump, `f9edc9de2a99afb0e629e86f600b2c860e8394dd` first version assertions, `6fc01bdd8f76a6333293dbacafd3907ca9921fd1` final code/test commit.

**Actual verified CI:** GitHub Actions **41162857377 completed SUCCESS**, code SHA `6fc01bdd8f76a6333293dbacafd3907ca9921fd1`, NO failed steps, **138 stable + 99 Next = 237 Python tests PASS**, Gradle `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, source-matched Wine28 native package PASS, aapt package `com.charoitx`, versionCode `87`, versionName `11.2-next.0.56`, APK signer fingerprint `d6790e512d182a4642d9a558db2508a3e20e82b5f5aa54847fd6206e7d42a175` matching cached Next key. Signed APK artifact **13027808207** (`Charoite-Core-Next-Android-APK`, 375,075,944 bytes ZIP, nonexpired, SHA-256 of ZIP `c8ce8d8180cc8e64fa987bcdccf7b1ea9bc3fcc56d93f55d99796ad84d3fceb5`), download https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41162857377/artifacts/13027808207 . Source attribution artifact 13028166892, nonexpired.

**User next step:** Install one signed APK from the ZIP **in-place over existing `com.charoitx`**, never uninstall/clear data or touch `com.charoite` stable Portal. Open System → Automatic phone diagnostics (GitHub opt-in), choose Preview, enter SAME regenerated fine-grained GitHub Issues token ONLY on phone (do not recreate unnecessarily), Enable. The actual phone encryption path, GitHub POST response and issue-intake triage workflow remain UNVERIFIED until a successful device result. **Automation caveat:** This achieves only opt-in diagnostic reporting, NOT a persistent autonomous developer or zero-touch APK installation. Wine28 child bootstrap remains blocked at `get_new_process_info success=0 exit_code=1` / `kernel32 c0000135`; no new Wine bootstrap performed.

**New pinned-source diagnosis:** Independently checked Wine10 pinned `The412Banner/proton-wine` source commit `c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767`. In `dlls/ntdll/unix/loader.c`, `load_apiset_dll()` opens **`dll_dir + get_pe_dir(current_machine) + "/apisetschema.dll"`** when not in a build tree. This path does NOT use `WINEDLLPATH` and can therefore point to Android's APK-owned native ELF library layout instead of the source-verified scratch `runtime/lib/wine/aarch64-windows`. The current 26-component Wine28 bundle also omits `apisetschema.dll`, as confirmed by its strict PE inventory. The missing schema is a real prerequisite gap, though the `kernel32 c0000135` root cause is still unproved and a file-presence-only fix would be insufficient.

The CI-only task compiles the exact pinned Wine `dlls/apisetschema/aarch64-windows/apisetschema.dll` target (original `-Wb,--data-only` source) in the already isolated Wine source build tree; verifies a Windows ARM64/ARM64EC PE32+ with a single correct namespace-v6 `.apiset` section, SHA-256, GPL/LGPL provenance and source `apisetschema.spec`, and publishes a separate source-audited **non-installable** artifact `Charoite-Wine-APISet-Schema-Experimental`. This is NOT added to the existing Next APK and NOT executed on a phone. [Source workflow Actions run 41402267742](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41402267742) launched; its final result and artifact must be checked independently (do not claim success before passing).

**Next safe changes after build verification:** In a NEW separately versioned source and PE/native cohort, add the verified apisetschema to a matching scratch layout and patch the Android ARM64 `load_apiset_dll()` native search to use an explicit verified scratch source path, with fail-closed absolute-path checks and no changes to the legacy Linux Wine fallback. Rebuild **all dependent native and PE components coherently**, update manifests/staging, and test offline syntax/ABI/section hashes before requesting a **separate, opt-in, one-shot NEW scratch** Android Wineboot check. ## 153. November 3–4, 2026 — Wine29 .0.7 first native --version phone failure diagnosed and v0.62 fix

**Actual OWNER phone report on signed `com.charoitx` v0.61 (versionCode 92):** `CHAROITE NEXT — ISOLATED WINE29 .0.7 NATIVE VERSION-ONLY GATE`. Prior `.0.6` archive remains untouched, new `.0.7` source archive and all three APK native ELF SHA-256 checks PASS; native loader process `--version` returned exit code **1** with exact output:
```
Wine29/Wine28 selector conflict or invalid native directory
wine: could not load ntdll.so: (null)
```
The UI reported `RESULT: BLOCKED — SecurityException: Wine29 --version exit=1`. **Wine29 native loader did run, but no successful version output; wineserver version was not attempted due to the first failure.** No PE/Windows EXE, Wineboot, prefix, FEX, graphics, games or user data accessed.

**Root cause verified in GitHub source (not speculative):** `next-generation/charoite-wine/prepare-wine29-native.py` injects loader/main.c guard `if (!native29 || !pe29 || !data29 || ...)` and rejects any incomplete `CHAROITE_WINE29_NATIVE_DIR`, `CHAROITE_WINE29_PE_DIR`, `CHAROITE_WINE29_DATA_DIR` triad before `try_dlopen(..., "libcharoite_wine29_ntdll.so")`. The old `CharoiteWine29Native07VersionGate.version()` cleared inherited environment and set ONLY `CHAROITE_WINE29_NATIVE_DIR`, causing the observed exact error. The `ntdll.so: (null)` message was downstream of guard rejection, NOT evidence of corrupt ELF or Windows NTDLL. **Never claim it proved Wine29 native executable success**, and do not mix older Wine28 native components.

**Fixed in GitHub:** `next-generation/CharoiteWine29Native07VersionGate.java` commit [c7dbb79](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/commit/c7dbb7924f6f0b5f8552696c5d1f3f6862f3f202). After `CharoiteWine29Native07Audit.run()` and independent per-ELF SHA verification, version-only `ProcessBuilder` now sets **ALL THREE** mandatory Wine29 source selectors (`CHAROITE_WINE29_NATIVE_DIR`, `CHAROITE_WINE29_PE_DIR`, `CHAROITE_WINE29_DATA_DIR`) to the **same canonical APK-owned native-library directory**. This is a deliberate `--version`-ONLY sentinel; the APK native directory is NOT a legitimate PE or NLS runtime layout, and no PE/NLS files are extracted, staged or executed. An explicit check blocks if `ntdll.dll`, `apisetschema.dll` or `nls` unexpectedly exists there; a real Windows PE/NLS load will still fail closed. `env.clear()` keeps inherited `HOME`, `WINEPREFIX`, LD_* and old Wine selectors out; only `PATH`, `WINEDEBUG=-all`, and the mandatory .0.7 triad are set. Two strict `--version` commands, 7s per-command timeout, bound output, exact output and exit check remain. No version code added to run Wineboot or initialize a prefix; no prior Wine28 scratch/user game data touched. **The already source-matched Wine29 .0.7 27-component/76-NLS binaries and original .0.6 cohort are UNMODIFIED.**

**v0.62 APK:** `next-generation/prepare_next.py` versionCode **93** / versionName **11.2-next.0.62**, same `com.charoitx` and existing signing key; Charoite Next Runtime status explicitly says the version-only triad is complete but Wineboot remains blocked. `next-generation/tests/test_wine29_native07_version_gate.py` now tests all 3 exact environment variable settings, checks real pinned Wine29 source requires them, blocks `WINEPREFIX` assignment, and checks ProcessBuilder only starts after triad and APK directory validation. Older v0.61 pinned Next/Wine26/27/28 tests were updated to expect v0.62 with all safety assertions retained. GitHub workflow `.github/workflows/charoite-next.yml` now verifies all three selector settings before Android Gradle compilation. Two initial CI runs failed exclusively from stale v0.61 tests and a regression-test false positive reading the WORD `WINEPREFIX` in a safety comment rather than an environment assignment; the last regression test correction [c9ffdd9](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/commit/c9ffdd90fa44f616166f21f107045f0a03acd6bd) fixed this. **No native Wine source/ELF recompilation needed for this exact triad mismatch.**

## 155. November 4, 2026 — v0.64 sealed PE/NLS private inventory and read-only audit, CI verified

**Owner request:** “Continue” from v0.63, with publicly accessible GitHub main and existing Android signing identity. This session accessed current repository and the true v0.63 GitHub Actions source/artifacts before making changes. No phone or Wineboot access, no old Wine28 v1–v9 scratch or prefixes touched; this is Android source + CI only.

**Root issue found:** Previous `CharoiteWine29Native07Stage.verifyFiles()` validated the 100 expected PE/NLS files by SHA but did not reject **unexpected extra files or directories inside a staged private slot**. Before any future execution experimentation, source data must be a closed-world, hash-verified allowlist rather than presence of known good files alongside arbitrary unknown content.

**Committed hardening and UI:** [3444ee4](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/commit/3444ee4e3a20595c62af517fde098e42cba2e1a2) updates `next-generation/CharoiteWine29Native07Stage.java` with bounded `verifyClosedInventory` traversal: only 100 expected module files and 4 exact approved directory nodes are accepted; links, extra files, extra directories, unknown file types and incomplete directories all fail closed. `verifyRootContents` permits only the expected finalized private child in a successful sealed read-only audit. New `verifyOnly` validates all 100 APK asset hashes before rehashing the sealed private staged 100-file set, creates no directories and starts no native process. Explicit staging `run` checks root is otherwise empty before staging, and seals both fresh and previously completed slots; it does not erase or repair quarantined data. All existing game, save, Wine28, rootfs, user prefixes and old Wine29 assets remain untouched. `next-generation/prepare_next.py` adds **System → Verify staged Wine29 0.7 data (read-only)** as a separate on-demand Copy report button. **Owner Galaxy report:** The newly introduced `System → Preflight Wine29 0.7 PE loader layout (read-only)` in CI-signed v0.65 returned exactly:
```

Wine28 v1–v9, stable Portal, games, saves and rootfs: NOT ACCESSED
```

This blocked inside the **static PE-header scanner**, after the independent hash-verified data audit returned PASS. It is NOT a new native Wine29 failure and does not invalidate the earlier on-phone version-only PASS, 24/76 staging PASS or 100-file sealed inventory PASS. The v0.65 error omitted the offending file/raw machine, so the phone report alone did not establish which DLL header caused it.

**Source-confirmed logic discrepancy:** The pinned `next-generation/charoite-wine/assemble-bootstrap-bundle.py` explicitly documents that hybrid ARM64X DLLs can have raw DOS/COFF `AA64` while their source-built ARM64EC view is `A641`. The independent `verify-bootstrap-bundle.py` deliberately permits either raw `0xAA64` or `0xA641` for source-verified ARM64EC components (API-set permits either with its own namespace-v6 verification), while insisting that ARM64 `wineboot.exe` raw header is **exactly `0xAA64`**. By contrast, the new v0.65 Java `CharoiteWine29Native07PePreflight.inspect()` incorrectly demanded a raw `0xA641` for **all** 23 DLLs, causing a false-positive architecture mismatch on valid hybrid raw headers. It would be wrong to patch the binary, disable SHA verification, or mix Wine28 components.
