## 161. November 4–5, 2026 — six source-built Wine29 ARM64EC DLL candidates VERIFIED in independent CI

**Owner request:** “Continue” after the signed v0.66 on-phone Wine29 static preflight PASS (Section 159) and independent 20-source-vs-five-phone import-owner audit PASS (Section 160). Continue direct GitHub main development, never paid OpenAI API, never wipe, uninstall, or touch existing stable Portal, Wine28 v1–v9, game saves, prefixes, rootfs. No owner consent was provided for Windows/Wineboot execution. The goal was to **compile separate, source-coherent missing PE candidates in GitHub Actions only** with no Android installation or runtime actions.

**Exact source and build:** Pinned The412Banner/proton-wine git SHA `c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767` and pre-existing Wine29 generation `wine10-arm64ec-bootstrap.0.7`, original verified 27 component + 76 NLS archive SHA-256 `66068032bd2203c8e53fddc92ce46b84086eb293306b2bd12bb741bf9a5e035e` from source run `41749167248`. The job reuses established `prepare-source.sh`, `prepare-wine29-native.py`, NDK r27d, checksum-pinned LLVM-MinGW 20261020, `build-arm64ec-ntdll.sh`, `verify-wine29-native-compile.py`, and `build-wine29-complete.sh`. The **independent newly created** runner source tree and immutable source generation are verified against the existing archive before extra candidate compilation. Candidate DLLs are published to an entirely separate GitHub Actions artifact, NOT copied into the old .0.7 bundle or on-phone sealed 100-file PE/NLS slot.

**Implemented files:** `next-generation/wine29-deps/build-wine29-deps.sh`, `verify-wine29-deps.py`, `test_wine29_deps.py`, and `.github/workflows/wine29-07-dependency-candidates.yml`. The new pipeline performs signed-off source validation, verifies original 27/76 source-matched baseline, cross-builds exactly `coml2`, `imm32`, `oleacc`, `oleaut32`, `shcore`, `newdev` from that same Wine source lineage, SHA-hashes each ELF/PE source provenance, inspects raw ARM64/ARM64EC PE headers, checks LLVM ARM64EC view, records direct DLL imports, and re-verifies the 6 *copied artifact bytes* and log outputs before artifact upload. All source-only, no Wine/Wineboot/PE invocation, no prefix/scratch/phone access.

**Failure/recovery evidence** (retain for reproducibility):
- First [run 41937252032](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41937252032): initial trivial CI safety test string mismatch; stopped before source compilation. The source contract was corrected in `dafe373`.
- Next [run 41937323311](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41937323311): all original native/27 baseline source verification PASS; candidate `oleaut32.dll` failed ARM64EC compilation due to Intel x87 instructions `fnclex`, `fstcw`, `fldcw`, `fldl`, `fmull`, `fistpll`, `fstsw` emitted by `dlls/oleaut32/vartype.c` `VarCyFromR8`. The ARM64EC hybrid toolchain exposes x86-compatible macros but emits AArch64 code. This is a C source architecture branch error, not missing DLL and **not a failed phone Wine29 run**.
- The targeted patch, committed in `047b4d7` with independent proof tests `e9496e3` and `8b65b5c`, adds one `!defined(CHAROITE_ARM64EC_PORTABLE_CURRENCY)` condition to the exact pinned x87 source branch and adds `-DCHAROITE_ARM64EC_PORTABLE_CURRENCY` to the fresh candidate-only ARM64EC compiler wrapper, selecting Wine's existing non-x87 `VARIANT_DutchRound` fallback. This can differ at **currency rounding boundaries**, so it is an experimental portability adaptation and must not be presented as byte-identical Windows arithmetic or production behavior. `portable-currency-proof.json` stores original/new source SHA-256, compiler define, path, and scope; the original 27-component baseline was assembled *before* this adaptation.
- [Run 41938342778](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/41938342778): source patch was accepted and **all six candidates successfully cross-compiled**, but the independent replay auditor detected a path difference between LLVM logs generated against build-tree paths and re-inspection of the copied output files. | DLL | Source-only first successful build | Independent successful build | Differences | SHA-256 after normalizing exactly both matching PE timestamps |
|---|---|---|---|---|

Raw file bytes are **not bit-identical without timestamp normalization**. The exact same 4-byte timestamp values occur twice within each respective binary and differ by build time. Zeroing only the two 4-byte timestamp slots in both builds yielded byte-for-byte equality with normalized hashes above. This is strong evidence of repeatable *substantive code bytes* for these two modules, not proof of runtime correctness or Windows arithmetic equivalence.

```text
  deferred: advpack.dll

  deferred: cryptnet.dll

```

**Interpretation:** The owner-confirmed 111-file staging, read-only hashes, and now PE header/import graph exactly match source-attested expectations. The AA64 raw COFF values are the known carrier form of ARM64EC-view hybrid DLLs and do not imply 34 standalone A641 files. The 15 delayed imports may be lazy-loaded and must not automatically become a 15-DLL build task without observed runtime failures; zero unbundled ordinary PE imports is NOT proof of successful dynamic native/PE loading or prefix creation. Original .0.7 ELF and sealed .0.12 PE/NLS contents have been verified in their distinct locations; **they have not been combined or executed as a Wine29 runtime**.

**Read-only evidence complete; next executable milestone NOT authorized by this report:** Assess an explicitly confirmed, one-shot bounded Wine29 PE-help/Wineboot bootstrap probe, following precautions established in historical Wine28 v8/v9 experiments (one new unique no_backup scratch path; original .0.7-native ELF hash gate plus 111-file .0.12 read-only PE/NLS SHA-256 gate before any write; fresh prefix and HOME/TMP/WINEPREFIX; matched native selector and PE/NLS search roots; exact source-hash copying; bounded server/PE time and logs; no auto retries, data cleanup, games, stable Portal, rootfs or existing prefixes accessed; retain failures for forensics). Wine may **auto-initialize a new scratch prefix even when invoked as wineboot.exe --help**: this must be communicated and separately authorized before device execution. In-app runtime execution must never be automatic. GitHub code planning/review is distinct from permission to execute on the phone. No successful Wineboot, Windows executable, Steam/Battle.net, FEX, graphics or gaming support has yet been shown.

## 182. November 6, 2026 — phone v0.75 kernel32 evidence and pinned bootstrap-source gate PASS

**Phone evidence:** The owner ran only the v0.75 read-only inspection of preserved v1. Original prefix/system.reg and prefix/user.reg are files, but prefix/drive_c is absent. Five original PE copies (kernel32/kernelbase/ntdll/apisetschema/wineboot) pass source SHA-256 and have AA64 PE32+ headers. Preserved PE log: Wineboot PE mapped, NTDLL PE mapped and API-set loaded; no kernel32 mapping, load_dll for kernel32 returned c0000135 and terminated. Preserved server log: automatic Wineboot --init had already exited 1, two alternative APK Wineboot paths yielded NO_SUCH_FILE, followed by a successful scratch Wineboot file mapping. Those alternative misses are NOT proof of missing kernel32.

**Static source audit:** [GitHub run 42320572066](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/42320572066) completed SUCCESS at commit d40395040978a603add631cc2cba20a618bfffc0, separate no-APK workflow. It verifies exact Wine upstream source commit c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767 using three pinned Git blobs, hashes for Wine29 .0.7 source archive and native patch, and the original Java v1 environment. All 11 guards PASS; machine-readable [artifact 13552314392](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/42320572066/artifacts/13552314392) (expires 2026-12-07). New code: analysis/wine29_bootstrap/audit_bootstrap.py and isolated workflow .github/workflows/wine29-bootstrap-source-audit.yml. The check executes no Wine, Wineboot, wineserver or PE, and accesses no phone data.

**Interpretation:** Wine 10 normally passes NULL load_path into its initial load_dll(kernel32). This does NOT mean WINEDLLPATH is absent. Wine's internal automatic --init temporarily receives WINEBOOTSTRAPMODE. Pinned Windows ntdll checks this flag when deciding whether to search WINEDLLDIR roots for built-in DLLs in an incomplete prefix. Unix ntdll restores the previous bootstrap flag before running the separate executable; original v1 Java cleanEnv() left it unset. Consequently, the later explicit --help failure is CONSISTENT with missing bootstrap-mode built-in fallback on an incomplete prefix, but this is NOT proven on-device. The *earlier* bootstrap-mode automatic --init failure (exit 1) is unresolved separately. Functional Wineboot and prefix remain unproven.

**Next:** Keep v1 scratch and all user data preserved. Design a fresh isolated and independently authorized v2 test to trace both --init and --help module resolution, bootstrap-mode presence and exact status. Do NOT globally turn on WINEBOOTSTRAPMODE in ordinary games or enable new engine switching from this source evidence. Signed Next remains v0.75, versionCode 106, [Actions APK artifact 13552056677](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/42318373542/artifacts/13552056677); this CI source audit produced NO new APK.

## 185. November 7, 2026 — v3 PE-help PASS, auto Wineboot child creation NTSTATUS 0x1, verified signed v0.78 read-only forensic build

**Owner's v3 Galaxy S26 Ultra readout:** Wine29 34-DLL header/import preflight, 111 staged PE/NLS, 3 APK-native ELF source hashes all PASS. Source-hash-verified ARM64 `wineboot.exe` launched specifically from `runtime/lib/wine/aarch64-windows/wineboot.exe` next to verified kernel32.dll. Native wineserver socket/lock PASS. In contrast to v1/v2, the explicit Wineboot --help exited **0**, printed `Usage: wineboot`, mapped and attached kernel32, kernelbase and supporting PE DLLs, and showed no `c0000135`. The hypothesis that v2's explicit process looked for kernel32 beside the incorrect `arm64-windows/wineboot.exe` was experimentally supported; changing the explicit EXE location solved that --help failure, but does NOT prove an initialized Windows prefix or game support.

**Remaining blocker corrected at source level:** Native Wine 10 `dlls/ntdll/unix/env.c` `run_wineboot()` logs `failed to start wineboot %x` when its `NtCreateUserProcess()` return status is nonzero, **before** it can resume a child process. Therefore the observed `run_wineboot failed to start wineboot 1` is an **NTSTATUS of hexadecimal 0x1**, not a Wineboot.exe child **exit code 1**. The exact reason for that Windows child-creation status in the patched Android Wine29 cohort is not established. V3 also printed `Cannot set the dir to C:\\windows (2)`, consistent with still-incomplete C: layout, and a nonfatal-for-help native `ws2_32.so` lookup warning; the cause and impact on real prefix init remain unknown. These must not be treated as a verified root cause without adjacent preserved trace evidence.

**Current safe next step implemented:** A new `next-generation/CharoiteWine29V3AutoInitForensics.java` runs **read-only** against only existing private `charoite-next-wine29-pe-dir-v3` scratch. Reads bounded `v3-wine-pe-help.log` (2MiB max) and `v3-foreground-wineserver.log` (256KiB max), hash-verifies five named PE images and inspects only file types for v3 prefix `drive_c` and registry presence; no reading prefix contents, no Wine or wineserver, no process, no scratch creation/write/cleanup/retry. Reports bounded and environment-redacted context around `NtCreateUserProcess`, `run_wineboot`, C: directory lookup, missing Unix .so and successful PE mappings. UI in System: **Inspect preserved Wine29 v3 auto-init failure (read-only)**. The v3/v2 one-shot execution controls are both archived/hidden; v1 preserved read-only button remains. No additional one-shot v4 is authorized or enabled. Stable Portal, user games, Wine prefixes/rootfs, old v1/v2 scratch all untouched.

## 186. November 7, 2026 — Wine29 v3 auto-init child process failure classified; v0.79 v4 direct -i opt-in signed APK verified

**Phone evidence:** Owner ran the v0.78 **preserved-v3, read-only forensics**. Under `charoite-next-wine29-pe-dir-v3`, the prefix has `system.reg`/ `user.reg` files but `drive_c` is absent. Five named PE files match sealed source SHA-256 and COFF AA64. Bounded v3 PE log (80,711 bytes, 474 lines) showed one `NtCreateUserProcess(C:\\windows\\system32\\wineboot.exe --init)`, missing `windows\\system32` path in unfinished C:, `get_pe_file_info assuming aa64 builtin`, then `run_wineboot failed to start wineboot 1`. **The subsequent explicit source-verified `aarch64-windows/wineboot.exe --help` mapped kernel32, kernelbase, NTDLL etc and printed Usage with exit 0.** The native `ws2_32.so` warning did not prevent --help. Neither `drive_c` absence nor a missing native adjunct warning alone proves the original child failure cause.

**Correction of exit-status interpretation:** In pinned Wine 10 `dlls/ntdll/unix/env.c`, `run_wineboot()` logs the return from `NtCreateUserProcess()`; in pinned `dlls/ntdll/unix/process.c`, that call may wait for child loader startup, then read `reply->exit_code` from wineserver's `get_new_process_info` if `success=false`. Its `spawn_process()` forks a native child that calls `exec_wineloader()` before the Windows process actually starts. **Thus 'wineboot 1' means that a new child process failed during startup with reported status `0x1`; it is NOT a verified actual Wineboot --init exit code.** A failing native loader re-exec is a plausible but UNPROVEN hypothesis. Upstream pointers: `https://sources.debian.org/src/wine/10.0~repack-6/dlls/ntdll/unix/env.c` and `https://sources.debian.org/src/wine/10.0~repack-6/dlls/ntdll/unix/process.c`. Existing source-patched Wine29 native ELF files are immutable; no native runtime code altered.

**New v4 experimental isolated path:** Added `next-generation/CharoiteWine29V4DirectInitProbe.java` adapting the previously source-hash-gated v3 PE layout without touching old scratch. It refuses if `getNoBackupFilesDir()/charoite-next-wine29-direct-init-v4` exists; preflights 34 PE headers/ordinary+delay imports, the sealed 111 PE/NLS and 3 APK-native ELF identity, copies sealed PE/NLS into NEW scratch (34 hybrid DLL, source-verified ARM64 Wineboot.exe + matched aarch64-windows alias, 76 NLS). Starts source-verified native wineserver in a new private prefix; explicitly launches `aarch64-windows/wineboot.exe -i` from the known working matched PE directory, without globally setting `WINEBOOTSTRAPMODE`. Wine itself may also trigger internal automatic --init. Process deadline **45s** / PE log cap 2MiB, server bounded; redacts logs; observes `drive_c` and `system.reg`, `user.reg`, `userdef.reg` presence and exact PE exit independently. Stops ONLY v4 processes; retains scratch permanently with NO cleanup/retries. This may still fail in the native child-loader handoff; only a phone report can tell.

**System UI:** New visible **Wine29 v4 direct prefix initialization (one-shot)** button requires a separate explicit confirmation describing `-i` and possible automatic --init, new private scratch and non-destructive boundaries. Read-only v3 auto-init and v1 preserved forensics buttons remain. Old v2/v3 executed one-shot controls stay archived/hidden; stable Portal `com.charoite` not modified; no game, saver, rootfs, existing prefixes, Steam or Battle.net accessed. Version target `com.charoitx` `11.2-next.0.79` code 110. New safety tests `next-generation/tests/test_wine29_v4_direct_init_contract.py`, explicit CI gate, license-source bundle, and `docs/WINE29_V4_DIRECT_INIT_DESIGN.md`.

The Windows C: mapping and essential directory creation are working **PARTIALLY**; no successful native child process or runnable Windows environment has been proven. Do not repeat/reuse v5 scratch.

**Pinned Wine10 source findings:** Added `analysis/wine29_bootstrap/audit_child_loader.py` and `analysis/wine29_bootstrap/tests/test_child_loader_audit.py`, expanded existing `.github/workflows/wine29-bootstrap-source-audit.yml` to fetch **exact pinned source commit** `c74acb09d51b447f5d5ac56c87608d3ec565a767` and verify Git blob SHA-1 for NTDLL Unix `loader.c` (`2f978520af6c216586baa081caa4c1db38207fd4`) and Unix `process.c` (`01286e119067567d1a8c7d3c8d5d61407ab43750`). Native `init_paths` computes `wineloader` from native `ntdll.so` location / compiled bindir rather than Java `nativeLibraryDir`, exports `WINELOADER`. The `WINELOADERNOEXEC` env var only skips Wine's first pre-exec. `spawn_process` forks a child that calls `exec_wineloader`, then `loader_exec` attempts `execv` on that derived executable; the grandchild may `_exit(1)` if exec fails, and wineserver `get_new_process_info` can propagate `reply->exit_code`. This is a **source-supported candidate** for observed `status 1`, but **exact runtime path, exec errno and cause are unconfirmed**. `WINEDLLPATH` cannot independently fix native executable selection.

**Read-only audit CI verified:** [Actions 42383786893](https://github.com/rgarn023/CharoitePortal/actions/runs/42383786893) SUCCESS, code audit commit `7e5b3d4e174abf368a1248e3ca33575fdaed718e`, **6 unit tests PASS**, `WINE29_BOOTSTRAP_SOURCE_FLOW=PASS_READ_ONLY`, `WINE29_CHILD_LOADER_SOURCE_AUDIT=PASS_READ_ONLY`. It makes NO APK and performs NO Wine/Wineboot/wineserver/PE execution, phone storage reads/writes, new scratch or cleanup; v0.80 signed artifact `13567539490` on Actions `42358173055` remains current.
